                                                            <section id="c1b6da07" data-cmcom="text-media" class="s-block">
    <div class="grid-container">
        <div class="col-span-12 md:col-span-6 md:order-last mb-6 self-center">
                                                                                                    <div class="mx-auto" style="max-width: 549.61832061069px;">
                                                                                                    <img
                                data-sizes="auto"
                                data-srcset="https://www.cm.com/cdn/web/en/safeguard.png 550w"
                                alt="safeguard"
                                data-cmcom="text-media-image"
                                width="600"
                                height="655"
                                class="s-image--lazyload m-auto rounded-lg">
                                                </div>
                                                        </div>
        <div class="col-span-12 md:col-span-6 md:order-first mb-6 self-center">
                            <header>
                                                                    <h2 class="title">
                                Combat Fraud With Safeguard and Safeguard Plus
                            </h2>
                                    </header>
                        <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">As the digital landscape continues to expand, the importance of cybersecurity has never been greater. Safeguard is a suite of software-based features aimed to combat fraud in business messaging. How? Both rule-based and AI-powered.<br><br><strong>Safeguard</strong></p><p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">Put restrictions and rules in place for traffic flow, and recognize and monitor suspicious activity in traffic with tools like Destination Management, Rate Limiting, and IP whitelisting. </p><p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px"><strong>Safeguard Plus</strong></p><p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">State-of-the-art solution that leverages advanced machine learning to provide real-time protection against AIT with tools like Dynamic Traffic Profiling, Flexible Monitor and Block Modes, and clear and actionable data insights. </p>

            
            <a href="https://www.cm.com/glossary/what-is-safeguard/" class="mt-6 s-button s-button--primary" target="_blank" rel="noopener">More on Safeguard</a><a href="https://www.cm.com/glossary/what-is-safeguard-plus/" class="mt-6 s-button s-button--secondary" target="_blank" rel="noopener">More on Safeguard Plus</a>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

                                                            <section id="e5b667dd" data-cmcom="question-answer" class="s-block">
    <div class="grid-container">
        <header class="col-span-12 mb-4 md:col-start-3 md:col-span-8 text-center">
            <h2 class="title">
                Frequently Asked Questions About SMS
            </h2>
        </header>
    </div>
    <div class="grid-container">
        <div class="grid-row">
            <div class="col-span-12 md:col-span-10 md:col-start-2 lg:col-span-8 lg:col-start-3">
                <s-collapse-group :only-one-active="true" class="v-cloak--hidden">
                                            <s-collapse :index="0">
                            <div class="qa-trigger" v-s-collapse-toggle>
                                <div class="s-title--h4 qa-question">
                                    What Is an SMS API, and How Does It Work?
                                </div>
                                                                    <div class="qa-chevron">
                                        <img
                                            data-src="https://www.cm.com/build/assets/open-vertical-382b717e.svg"
                                            alt="open vertical icon"
                                            class="group-aria-[expanded='true']/collapse:rotate-180 s-icon-svg s-image--lazyload">
                                    </div>
                                                            </div>
                            <div v-s-collapse-content>
                                <div class="wsywig-content qa-answer">
                                    <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">An SMS API is a type of API that allows your business to integrate SMS (Short Message Service) messaging into your existing software platforms. SMS APIs enable you to send or receive SMS messages quickly and easily through any website or application.</p>
                                    <a href="https://www.cm.com/glossary/what-is-sms-api/" class="s-button s-button--tertiary" target="_blank" rel="noopener">More About SMS API</a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </s-collapse>
                                            <s-collapse :index="1">
                            <div class="qa-trigger" v-s-collapse-toggle>
                                <div class="s-title--h4 qa-question">
                                    How to Send Bulk SMS?
                                </div>
                                                                    <div class="qa-chevron">
                                        <img
                                            data-src="https://www.cm.com/build/assets/open-vertical-382b717e.svg"
                                            alt="open vertical icon"
                                            class="group-aria-[expanded='true']/collapse:rotate-180 s-icon-svg s-image--lazyload">
                                    </div>
                                                            </div>
                            <div v-s-collapse-content>
                                <div class="wsywig-content qa-answer">
                                    <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">Sending bulk SMS with the CM.com SMS API is simple. Our platform supports mass messaging, allowing you to reach a large audience with personalized content, scheduling options, and delivery tracking.</p>
                                    
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </s-collapse>
                                            <s-collapse :index="2">
                            <div class="qa-trigger" v-s-collapse-toggle>
                                <div class="s-title--h4 qa-question">
                                    What is SMS Marketing?
                                </div>
                                                                    <div class="qa-chevron">
                                        <img
                                            data-src="https://www.cm.com/build/assets/open-vertical-382b717e.svg"
                                            alt="open vertical icon"
                                            class="group-aria-[expanded='true']/collapse:rotate-180 s-icon-svg s-image--lazyload">
                                    </div>
                                                            </div>
                            <div v-s-collapse-content>
                                <div class="wsywig-content qa-answer">
                                    <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">·With <a target="_self" href="/sms/">SMS</a> marketing you can send your marketing messages directly to the phones of your customers. Promote your products, services, events, discounts, and more to a targeted audience via personalized text messages. Benefit from the impressive 98% open rate and higher click-through rate of SMS messaging.</p>
                                    <a href="https://www.cm.com/blog/sms-marketing-open-rate/" class="s-button s-button--tertiary">More About SMS Marketing</a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </s-collapse>
                                            <s-collapse :index="3">
                            <div class="qa-trigger" v-s-collapse-toggle>
                                <div class="s-title--h4 qa-question">
                                    What Is an SMS Gateway?
                                </div>
                                                                    <div class="qa-chevron">
                                        <img
                                            data-src="https://www.cm.com/build/assets/open-vertical-382b717e.svg"
                                            alt="open vertical icon"
                                            class="group-aria-[expanded='true']/collapse:rotate-180 s-icon-svg s-image--lazyload">
                                    </div>
                                                            </div>
                            <div v-s-collapse-content>
                                <div class="wsywig-content qa-answer">
                                    <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">An SMS Gateway enables you to send text messages to mobile phones all around the world. The Gateway is a joining point between an organization’s internal systems and the mobile networks, letting you send out SMS messages from your business without a mobile connection. Around 96% of business text messages today are sent by a software application rather than a human. To integrate this functionality into your application, you (or your developers) should implement communication with a SMS API.</p><p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px"></p>
                                    <a href="https://www.cm.com/glossary/what-is-sms-gateway/" class="s-button s-button--tertiary" target="_blank" rel="noopener">More About SMS Gateway</a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </s-collapse>
                                            <s-collapse :index="4">
                            <div class="qa-trigger" v-s-collapse-toggle>
                                <div class="s-title--h4 qa-question">
                                    Are SMS Messages Encrypted, and Is the Channel Secure?
                                </div>
                                                                    <div class="qa-chevron">
                                        <img
                                            data-src="https://www.cm.com/build/assets/open-vertical-382b717e.svg"
                                            alt="open vertical icon"
                                            class="group-aria-[expanded='true']/collapse:rotate-180 s-icon-svg s-image--lazyload">
                                    </div>
                                                            </div>
                            <div v-s-collapse-content>
                                <div class="wsywig-content qa-answer">
                                    <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">SMS text messages are not encrypted. SMS Service Providers like CM.com have <a target="_self" href="/press/safeguard-destination-management-release/">safeguards</a> in place to protect businesses and data. These safeguards can monitor traffic, set IP Address restrictions, limit rates, and other measures to ensure security. </p>
                                    
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </s-collapse>
                                            <s-collapse :index="5">
                            <div class="qa-trigger" v-s-collapse-toggle>
                                <div class="s-title--h4 qa-question">
                                    How Can I Integrate the CM.com SMS API?
                                </div>
                                                                    <div class="qa-chevron">
                                        <img
                                            data-src="https://www.cm.com/build/assets/open-vertical-382b717e.svg"
                                            alt="open vertical icon"
                                            class="group-aria-[expanded='true']/collapse:rotate-180 s-icon-svg s-image--lazyload">
                                    </div>
                                                            </div>
                            <div v-s-collapse-content>
                                <div class="wsywig-content qa-answer">
                                    <p style="text-align: undefined; padding-left: 0px">Integrating the CM.com SMS API is straightforward. You can access comprehensive documentation, code examples, and SDKs on our developer portal. Our support team is also available to assist you during the integration process.</p>
                                    <a href="https://www.cm.com/sms/sms-gateway-api/" class="s-button s-button--tertiary">More About Our SMS API</a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </s-collapse>
                                    </s-collapse-group>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

<script>
    /**
     * Determine the mobile operating system.
     * This function returns one of 'iOS', 'Android', 'Windows Phone', or 'unknown'.
     *
     * @returns {String}
     */
    function getMobileOperatingSystem() {
        var userAgent = navigator.userAgent || navigator.vendor || window.opera;

        // Windows Phone must come first because its UA also contains "Android"
        if (/windows phone/i.test(userAgent)) return "WindowsPhone";
        if (/android/i.test(userAgent)) return "Android";
        // iOS detection from: http://stackoverflow.com/a/9039885/177710
        if (/iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(userAgent) && !window.MSStream) return "iOS";

        return "unknown";
    }

    /**
     * Determine the operating system.
     * This function returns one of 'Windows', 'MacOS', 'UNIX', 'Linux' or 'unknown'.
     *
     * @returns {String}
     */
    function getOperatingSystem() {
        if (navigator.appVersion.indexOf("Win") != -1) return "Windows";
        if (navigator.appVersion.indexOf("Mac") != -1) return "MacOS";
        if (navigator.appVersion.indexOf("X11") != -1) return "UNIX";
        if (navigator.appVersion.indexOf("Linux") != -1) return "Linux";

        return "unknown";
    }

    /**
     * set conversation variables
     *
     * @param {object} cmwcInstance
     */
    function setConversationContext(cmwcInstance) {

        if(!window.conversationContext)
        {
            window.conversationContext = {
                geoIP                : 'US',
                country              : '',
                websiteLanguage      : 'en',
                mobileOperatingSystem: getMobileOperatingSystem(),
                operatingSystem      : getOperatingSystem(),
                
                            };
        }

        // Add Tracedock to agent context asynchronously. 
        // It will be present in future contexts if not in time for the first call.
        window.cookieStore.get("anonymous_id").then(cookie => {
            if (cookie && cookie.value) {
                window.conversationContext.anonymous_id = cookie.value;
                console.log("Tracedock id added to context");
            }
        }).catch(e => {
            console.error("Error fetching tracedock id", e);
        });

        const storedData = sessionStorage.getItem('formData');
        const formData = (storedData) ? JSON.parse(storedData) : {};

        const context = Object.assign({}, window.conversationContext, formData);

        for (const i in context) {
            if (typeof context[i] === 'undefined' || context[i] === '') {
                delete context[i];
            }
        }        

        console.log("Conversation context set: ", context);
        cmwcInstance.addContext(context);

    }
</script>

<script>
    let CURRENT_LANGUAGE = "";

    function waitForElm(selector) {
        return new Promise(resolve => {
            if (document.querySelector(selector)) {
                return resolve(document.querySelector(selector));
            }

            const observer = new MutationObserver(mutations => {
                if (document.querySelector(selector)) {
                    observer.disconnect();
                    resolve(document.querySelector(selector));
                }
            });

            observer.observe(document.body, {
                childList: true,
                subtree: true
            });
        });
    }

    function addCustomStyling() {
        waitForElm('[configkey="3634145b-e3e3-4118-b645-3c3d7a222298"]').then((elm) => {
            const style = document.createElement('style');

            style.textContent = `
                .wc-c-root {
                    --wc-c-root-z-index: 69 !important;
                    max-height: calc(100svh - 80px);
                }
                `;
            elm.shadowRoot.appendChild(style);
        });
    }

    function hideNotification() {
        waitForElm('[instancekey]').then((elm) => {
            const style = document.createElement('style');
            style.textContent = `
                div[aria-live="assertive"][role="log"] {
                    display: none !important;
                }
            `;
            elm.shadowRoot.appendChild(style);
        });
    }

    function cmwcCallback() {
        setConversationContext(cmwc.get('3634145b-e3e3-4118-b645-3c3d7a222298'));
    }
</script>

</script>
